Status: NEW scoped completion only where this Part01 names missing creature-action details; existing combat, roll, defense, condition and damage/Null contracts retain their actual scopes. These procedures add no creature, rating, level, skill proficiency, spell, material, Action, Reaction or resource. Printed HP/AC/abilities/type/CR, every printed action and all original dossier text remain exact. The three tactic-only additions are proposed actions, not silently relabelled printed actions. Use the actual body and legal perception. Each new anatomical melee attack has reach5ft, measured between actual occupied body boundaries with a clear contact/line-of-effect witness; it is physical and nonspell. Pressure Wave instead uses its own declared actual body emitter and range. Full target validation precedes commitment and repeats before the particular outcome; a known illegal attempt spends nothing. After legal commitment, a genuinely authorized interruption can invalidate the still-pending outcome; spent Action/attack slot is not refunded and that outcome is not retargeted. No new interrupt window or free Reaction is supplied. Ordinary Ready remains trigger-first. One standalone action spends one real Action. A permitted Multiattack spends one Action once, resolves its two selections in order, and revalidates each selection independently; every performed selection consumes its slot even on a miss/save success. No second Action, Bonus Action or Reaction is created. Skipping an unusable remaining slot grants no refund. Use binary saves and actual printed attack modifiers; no PB is inferred from CR, owner level or save proficiency. Attack natural1 misses; natural20 hits and doubles only damage dice. Saves have no generic natural1/20 exception. Roll each real damage event separately. Original source actor is the dealer, not an assumed controller; create one source-qualified root per resolved damage event and retain any actually qualifying creation-time Null flag. Pure transfer preserves provenance and does not replay the action or its control rider. Apply actual defenses once, then temporary HP and normal HP, then existing instant-death/Wound/zero-state and concentration consequences in their actual order. Wounds are tested per packet, never by summing Multiattack packets; a qualifying Null root uses its own accepted exceptions. None of these actions gains Maiming or an unprinted Wound exemption. Resolve a new on-hit save or post-damage movement/condition only while its same recipient remains an eligible creature. Preventing damage is not automatically preventing an independently admitted non-damage rider. These actions do not manufacture equipment damage, loot, recharge, a harvest slot or a new corpse. At actual death use the recipient's actual lifecycle; no general monster resurrection/remains rule is authored here. Ordinary forced movement spends no voluntary movement, provokes no ordinary opportunity attack, and stops at the first obstacle that prevents legal whole-body placement. It does not grant passage through another body, collision damage or Hover. Actual reach breaks end grapples; loss of support uses the existing Falling contract. Mechanical impairment is distinct from damage immunity. Repeated callback delivery cannot duplicate any committed slot, root, movement or application: retain action/subattack/event and condition-source identifiers. The numerical cases instantiate synthetic legal scene inputs, not authenticated campaign creatures, proficiencies, class access, resources or schedules. They are authored calculations, not project-engine execution. Bodies and slots supplied in those fixtures do not settle BV3-16 formation composition or other creatures' geometry. GOV 4 ## Maneuvers and ordinary attacks All opposed checks use Core total comparison: higher wins, exact tie preserves state. No natural-20 automatic victory or hidden task-degree effect on an opposed tie. A printed save-based class maneuver keeps its own method. | Maneuver | Execution | |---|---| | Grapple | Replace one Attack Action attack; reach and target ≤one size larger. STR Athletics vs defender STR Athletics/DEX Acrobatics. Escape Action uses same opposed contest. NEW one free usable grasping hand/appendage per target; release freely on own turn. Ends if required reach breaks or grappler becomes Incapacitated. No concentration required. Dragging halves Speed unless target ≥two sizes smaller. Magical/card exceptions remain source-specific. | | Shove | Replace one attack; same reach/size/contest limits; success pushes 5 ft away or knocks Prone. | | Trip | Replace one melee attack, Athletics vs Athletics/Acrobatics, success Prone. NEW same one-size-larger cap. | | Disarm | Replace one attack, Athletics or weapon check vs Athletics/Acrobatics. Item must be held and reachable. Attacker chooses legal adjacent unoccupied drop space, otherwise target's own space. Explicit non-droppable bonds prevent it; magic/curse alone does not. No severed attunement/ownership/curse. | | Feint | Bonus Action Deception vs Insight; success advantage on next melee attack this turn only. | | Sunder | Attack an object/shield/armor component/construct joint using its actual durability system rather than creature HP. Unspecified durability remains an equipment dependency. | | Break Formation | Special Shove replacing one attack, same reach/size contest with attacker disadvantage. Success pushes 10 ft; snapshot target's adjacent allies before push and remove their Guard Action benefits. Does not remove Guarded stance or target's own Guard. | | Overwatch | Ready one ranged attack against movement in a named lane. Reaction and ammunition paid at release. Distinct from Soldier's class engine. | **Pin and Silence the Caster — NEW tradeoff.** Declare before rolling and take **−2 to that attack roll**. Each creature has one Pin token and one Silence the Caster token between starts of its regular turns. An unused token may be spent on any otherwise legal qualifying attack, including off-turn, extra-turn or readied attacks. A legal declaration followed by an attack roll consumes the token even on a miss; an illegal declaration rejected before rolling consumes nothing. Tokens refresh only at regular own-turn start, never on an extra turn or numbered-round boundary. One attack cannot combine another optional tactical maneuver. These provisional numbers require table playtesting. Pin uses a ranged weapon attack. On hit deal ordinary damage and apply Speed −10 (floor 0), DC `8 + PB + attack ability modifier`. Ordinary Pins do not stack the penalty. At target's regular start, one optional STR Athletics task check against the highest active ordinary Pin DC ends all ordinary Pins on Success/Critical Success. Alternatively one Action removes all automatically. Source-specific card Pins do not silently acquire this generic definition or removal scope. Silence the Caster uses a melee attack. On hit prevent verbal-component Reactions until target's next regular start. If concentrating, make exactly one CON concentration save at `max(10, floor(post-mitigation damage/2))`, including DC10 when damage is zero. This fulfills, rather than duplicates, the normal damage concentration check. It is not Core Silenced: speech and Action-time verbal casting remain possible. **Interpose.** While Guarding, after an adjacent ally becomes an attack target but before its roll, spend Reaction and become target if legal for that attack (range/type/line/cover). NEW may move **up to** 5 ft using a separate terrain-paying budget, including zero if already legally positioned. Speed0 permits the zero-movement case only. It redirects one attack without cancelling or duplicating it. Guard's unspent +2 applies if this is the first attack against you. Voluntary movement has ordinary opportunity-attack consequences. **Press.** On a melee hit during your regular turn, arm one target until next regular start. While unconsumed, another qualifying hit during that turn may replace it. If the target voluntarily retreats from an adjacent space, follow up to 5 ft into the vacated space using a separate terrain-paying budget; no Action/BA/Reaction. It can provoke ordinary opportunity attacks. No following teleport/forced movement. Once used, cannot rearm before next regular turn, including during an extra turn. Speed0 prevents movement. **Opportunity attack.** Spend Reaction for one melee attack against a seen hostile creature voluntarily leaving reach, immediately **before** exit. Forced movement/teleport do not trigger; Disengage suppresses it for its stated turn. Specific reviewed triggers may override these conditions. **Two-weapon fighting.** After taking Attack and attacking with a Light melee weapon in one hand, spend Bonus Action for one attack with a different Light melee weapon in the other hand. Do not add a positive ability modifier to that attack's damage; include a negative modifier. This grants neither another Bonus Action nor a class feature. GOV 5 ## Movement and geometry Track **spent movement cost**, not distance, for ordinary turn movement. Mode cap = `(1 + number of legal Dash actions this turn) × current Speed for that mode`; remaining = max(0, cap − spent cost). Switching modes never refunds cost. Recalculate cap on Speed changes, preserving spent cost. Stand from Prone costs floor(half current Speed) and requires nonzero Speed and enough budget. Dropping Prone costs no movement. For each foot traveled, normal/difficult/severe terrain costs 1/2/3; use strongest terrain only. Add +1 for crawling and +1 for squeezing. Crawl in difficult =3:1; crawl+squeeze in difficult =4:1. A nonhostile occupied space counts as difficult terrain. Enter a hostile creature's space only with ≥two-size difference or an express feature; never willingly end in an occupied space. Squeeze through a space one size smaller, with own attack/DEX-save disadvantage and attacks-against advantage. Walk10 through difficult spends20; switch to Fly30 leaves10. Stand with Speed30 spends15; switching to Fly30 leaves15. Two legal Dashes at Speed30 give cap90; prior expenditure still counts. Interpose/Press grant5 budget: they cannot enter a difficult 5-ft tile costing10. Their separate budgets never refill ordinary movement. Ready movement uses Speed at release; Withdraw uses half current Speed. All voluntary movement requires nonzero relevant Speed. Forced movement does not use these ledgers or provoke ordinary opportunity attacks, cannot place creatures in solid matter, and stops at obstacles. Teleport does not traverse intervening terrain or spend Speed unless printed; it requires a legal destination. Speed0 alone does not block forced movement or teleport. Flight with Prone/Speed0/lost support causes Falling unless Hover or an explicit magical support exception applies; use the existing fixed Fall Marker procedure. Reaching support ends Falling. NEW grid metric: each orthogonal or diagonal neighboring tile is 5 ft; use the same metric for ordinary ranges/areas. No diagonal cut through a corner when both adjacent orthogonal sides are completely blocked. Exact templates, 3D geometry, mounts, chases, jumping, climbing/swimming procedures and currents need further source review. GOV 7 ## Rolls Choose the roll type before rolling. Use the actual ability modifier, including negative values, and normal proficiency only when applicable. Existing advantage/disadvantage chooses the higher/lower of two d20s; multiple sources do not stack, and any advantage plus disadvantage cancels. Add other legal modifiers once. Fractions round down unless a rule specifies otherwise. One tile is 5 feet. Class L, S and effect M retain their definitions in the shared class rules. **Attack:** roll d20 plus the appropriate attack modifier against AC. Meeting AC hits. Natural 1 always misses; natural 20 always hits and critically doubles damage dice, not flat modifiers. Beating AC by 10 is not a critical hit. An explicit broader critical range does not stack with another; use the broadest eligible range. In this overlay, a natural result below 20 in that range must still meet AC unless the feature expressly says it always hits. **Task, skill or tool check against a fixed DC:** total at least DC+10 is Critical Success; at least DC is Success; at most DC-10 is Critical Failure; other totals below DC are Failure. Natural 20 then improves one degree and natural 1 worsens one, bounded at either end. A task printing only success/failure collapses the two upper degrees to success and two lower degrees to failure; no extra consequence is invented. A natural 20 can still fail an otherwise impossible check, and a natural 1 with sufficient modifiers can still succeed. **Saving throw:** total at least DC succeeds; otherwise it fails. Natural 1 and 20 have no generic special outcome. A printed failure by 5 or 10 compares the final total with the DC; it does not add an unprinted critical-save system. Death saves are the specific exception below. **Opposed check, new provisional tie rule:** compare final totals. Higher wins; an exact tie leaves the existing state unchanged, so the initiating creature fails to change it. Do not turn task natural shifts into hidden numeric modifiers. A maneuver explicitly printing degree outcomes must specify how those apply. GOV 11 ## Grappled **Preserved Core text:** Speed 0; ends if grappler is incapacitated or reach is broken. **Effect:** Speed becomes 0 and cannot be increased by bonuses while Grappled. **Application:** A Grappled application records the grappler/source and its reach. **Duration and ending:** Ends if the grappler is Incapacitated, reach breaks, or the applying effect ends/its escape succeeds. A class/spell card with a fixed save DC uses an Action and STR (Athletics) or DEX (Acrobatics) against that DC. An ordinary V6 grapple uses an Action to escape with STR (Athletics) or DEX (Acrobatics) opposed by the grappler's STR (Athletics); higher total wins, ties retain the grapple. Multiple grapplers require ending each relationship. **Stacking:** Multiple grapplers may maintain separate Grappled records; Speed remains 0 until all relevant grapples end. **Immunities and specific exceptions:** A creature immune to Grappled cannot receive it. Teleportation or forced movement that breaks reach ends that grapple unless a specific effect says otherwise. **Compatible V6/source details:** - V6 adds that the creature cannot benefit from bonuses to Speed and confirms ending when the grappler is incapacitated or an effect removes the target from reach. - V6 all-XML paragraph 8206 gives ordinary grapple application and opposed escape; fixed-DC card escapes remain specific class/spell rules. **Remaining wider integration work:** - Final generic grapple/shove procedure remains part of the combat maneuver audit. Provenance: Core V2 `books/player/Decennium_Descent_Core_Systems_Bible_v2_Known_Descent.docx`, zero-based all-XML heading paragraph 747, definition paragraph 748; both positions, exact text and book SHA-256 are verified against the approved book and retained in the [condition data](../../data/playtest/condition_playtests.json). <a id=\"incapacitated\"></a> GOV 12 ## Prone **Preserved Core text:** Crawl or stand; melee attackers within 5 ft. have advantage; ranged attackers have disadvantage. **Effect:** Own attacks have disadvantage. Melee attacks against it within 5 feet have advantage; ranged attacks against it have disadvantage at every distance; other attacks beyond 5 feet have disadvantage. Movement requires crawling or standing. Drop Prone without Speed expenditure; standing costs half current Speed, rounded down, requires nonzero Speed and sufficient movement. Crawl costs 2 feet per foot, or 3 in difficult terrain. **Application:** Applied when an effect explicitly knocks a creature Prone or another rule such as Unconscious causes it. **Duration and ending:** Ends when the creature successfully stands or an explicit effect ends Prone. **Stacking:** Does not stack. **Immunities and specific exceptions:** Prone immunity blocks application. A feature may explicitly ignore Prone attack penalties. **Compatible V6/source details:** - V6: only movement is crawl unless standing; own attack rolls have disadvantage; attacks from within 5 feet have advantage, otherwise disadvantage. **Source conflicts:** - For a ranged attack made from within 5 feet, V6 distance-only wording would grant advantage while Core specifically says ranged attackers have disadvantage. Core controls. **Explicit integration decisions:** - Core-vs-V6 conflict resolved in favor of Core for ranged attacks within 5 feet. - Recovered V6 movement: half-Speed standing and 2/3-for-1 crawling. NEW explicit zero-Speed standing prohibition, preventing zero-cost standing while Grappled. Provenance: Core V2 `books/player/Decennium_Descent_Core_Systems_Bible_v2_Known_Descent.docx`, zero-based all-XML heading paragraph 763, definition paragraph 764; both positions, exact text and book SHA-256 are verified against the approved book and retained in the [condition data](../../data/playtest/condition_playtests.json). <a id=\"resonance-burn\"></a>"
